# Sunnsteel Product Roadmap

Last verified against the frontend, backend, and shared contracts: **2026-09-22**.

This is the canonical product feature registry for Sunnsteel. It tells humans and
coding agents what the product already does, what is next, which ideas are only
candidates, and which foundations must exist before larger features are started.

The shared [product vision](../../../PRODUCT_VISION.md) defines enduring
direction and principles. It guides trade-offs but does not approve, schedule or
mark features as shipped; this roadmap owns those decisions and states.

This document is not a technical-debt log. Performance defects, cleanup, and
implementation hazards belong in [technical-debt.md](technical-debt.md). The code
remains the final source of truth when it disagrees with this file.

## How to use this document

Before proposing or implementing product work:

1. Read the current product snapshot and active queue.
2. Find the feature by its stable ID; do not create a duplicate entry.
3. Verify the relevant code before changing a status to `SHIPPED`.
4. Claim an item before implementation starts: on `main`, move it to
   `IN_PROGRESS` and add a row to [In-flight work](#in-flight-work). Never start
   an item that already has a claim there. See that section for the rules.
5. When it ships, add the completion date and links to the relevant code, PR, or
   migration in the completion log.
6. If a feature changes API shapes, update and publish `@sunsteel/contracts`
   before consuming the new version in the frontend or backend.

### Statuses

| Status        | Meaning                                                           |
| ------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `SHIPPED`     | Verified in the current code.                                     |
| `IN_PROGRESS` | Actively being implemented.                                       |
| `NEXT`        | Selected as the immediate product work.                           |
| `QUEUED`      | Approved direction with an established order.                     |
| `CANDIDATE`   | Worth retaining, but not committed to the queue.                  |
| `BLOCKED`     | Cannot proceed until a stated dependency or decision is resolved. |
| `DEFERRED`    | Intentionally postponed.                                          |
| `REJECTED`    | Evaluated and declined; retain the reason.                        |

Repository abbreviations: **FE** = `sunnsteel-frontend`, **BE** =
`sunnsteel-backend`, **CT** = `sunnsteel-contracts`.

### Sizes

Size is structural, not an estimate: it is derived from how far a change reaches,
not from how long anyone thinks it takes. It exists so the queue cannot hide a
quarter of work behind a group that looks like the one before it.

| Size | Reach                                                                                                             |
| ---- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `S`  | One repository, no contract publish, no migration, no new model.                                                  |
| `M`  | One or two repositories, may publish contracts, no data migration.                                                |
| `L`  | Cross-repository with a schema change, migration or backfill, or a new surface others depend on.                  |
| `XL` | A new subsystem, or work gated on policy, moderation, licensed content or infrastructure that does not exist yet. |

An `XL` is a signal to decompose before scheduling, not a licence to start.

## In-flight work

More than one agent works on this product at the same time, each in its own git
worktree. A status changed only inside a feature branch is invisible to everyone
else until it merges, so claims live here, on `main`.

- **Claim before you start.** On `main`, set the item's status to `IN_PROGRESS`
  (or `NEXT` when reserving it for your next slice) and add a row below. Do this
  before creating the worktree.
- **Never start a claimed item**, and avoid items whose repositories and modules
  overlap a claimed one. Pick something else or ask the owner.
- **Release the claim when the item ships or is abandoned.** Remove the row in
  the same change that marks it `SHIPPED`, or revert the status if the work
  stops.
- **`@sunsteel/contracts` versions are serialized.** When two claims both need a
  publish, the second to publish rebases on the first and takes the next version.

| ID      | Status        | Owner  | Branch / worktree | Claimed    | Repositories |
| ------- | ------------- | ------ | ----------------- | ---------- | ------------ |
| ROUT-16 | `IN_PROGRESS` | Claude | `claude/rout-16`  | 2026-09-23 | CT, BE, FE   |

## Immediate product-integrity work

These are current correctness or honesty gaps, not optional enhancements.

They are split by size on purpose. The first table is frontend-only work that
removes false statements from screens that already exist; it can ship as one
change with no contract publish and no backend. The second table holds gaps that
a feature owns instead: either the small honest fix is a step toward it, or --
as with `FIX-05` -- there is no honest short version and the surface stays
unfinished until the real capability lands. Do not let the first table wait for
the second.

The first table shipped on 2026-09-07 and is retained as a record of what was
corrected and where. `FIX-03` then closed the remaining actionable integrity gap;
`FIX-05` shipped with its owning public-profile capability in `PROF-12`.

### Honesty fixes — frontend only

| ID     | Status    | Size | Work item                          | Expected behavior                                                                                                                                                                                               | Repositories |
| ------ | --------- | ---- | ---------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------ |
| FIX-01 | `SHIPPED` | S    | Remove fabricated profile content  | Profiles must not claim that every user has the same biography or lives in San Francisco. Show real optional data or an honest empty state.                                                                     | FE           |
| FIX-02 | `SHIPPED` | S    | Remove the unconditional Pro badge | Display a membership/title badge only when backed by a real account state.                                                                                                                                      | FE           |
| FIX-06 | `SHIPPED` | S    | Stop implying interactive counts   | Follower and Following counts must not render as interactive while they lead nowhere. Browsable relationship lists, and the paginated endpoint they need, belong to `SOC-02`.                                   | FE           |
| FIX-07 | `SHIPPED` | S    | Align search copy with behavior    | Do not claim username search until unique usernames exist; avoid presenting email as a public identity.                                                                                                         | FE           |
| FIX-08 | `SHIPPED` | S    | Replace fixed dashboard goals      | Label weekly and lifetime targets as generic milestones until `PROG-08` supplies real user goals.                                                                                                               | FE           |
| FIX-09 | `SHIPPED` | S    | Unify duration formatting          | `formatDuration` is now the single human-readable duration formatter across the active session, dashboard, history list and history detail; hour rollover keeps long sessions readable (for example, `22h 3s`). | FE           |
| FIX-10 | `SHIPPED` | S    | Fix day-count pluralisation        | Routine cards and the training-day wizard now share `formatDaysPerWeek`, so singular counts render as `1 day/week` while zero and multiple counts remain plural.                                                | FE           |

### Integrity gaps owned by a feature

| ID     | Status    | Size | Work item                          | Expected behavior                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                | Repositories         |
| ------ | --------- | ---- | ---------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | -------------------- |
| FIX-03 | `SHIPPED` | M    | Correct kg/lb handling             | Canonical storage remains kilograms (`SetLog.weight`, `PersonalRecord.weight`, `WorkoutAnalyticsProjection.totalVolumeKg`); the frontend converts only at display and entry boundaries. **Shipped 2026-09-07 in the required order:** the shared conversion boundary landed before Settings exposed the already-persisted `weightUnit`. Active logging, previous performance, routine detail/editor, history, dashboard, profile and body-weight settings now display the preference, while editable values convert back to kilograms before validation or persistence. A bounded tolerance absorbs only two-decimal pound round-trip noise and prevents a unit transition from autosaving unchanged sets. No backend, contract publish or migration was needed. | FE                   |
| FIX-04 | `SHIPPED` | S    | Hide the Quick Workout entry point | Decided 2026-09-07: hide the button rather than seed it with a starting exercise. It created a routine whose only day had an empty `exercises` array, so the session was unloggable. Hiding was reversible; the replacement is now claimed under `LIVE-06` as a picker over the owner's existing routine days, without restoring the broken empty-session path.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  | FE                   |
| FIX-05 | `SHIPPED` | -    | Make Share Profile functional      | Shipped 2026-09-10 with `PROF-12`: Share Profile copies a real `/members/<username>` URL, confirms success, reports clipboard failure, and sends signed-out recipients to the privacy-filtered public profile instead of Login.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  | FE, BE via `PROF-12` |
| FIX-11 | `SHIPPED` | S    | Make Forgot password functional    | Shipped 2026-09-13: Login's "Forgot password?" now opens a real reset. The request page emails a Supabase recovery link without revealing whether an account exists; the link returns through the existing `/auth/callback` to a page that sets the new password, and an expired or used link comes back to the request form with an explanation. The missing route had logged a 404 on every production Login visit.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            | FE                   |

| 6     | Progress destination     | L    | Complete — PROG-01 through PROG-08 shipped                                                                                      | Progress combines record frontiers, session history, muscle distribution, bounded workload, same-day session comparisons, a global event timeline and private measurable goals.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| 7     | Achievements and ranks   | L    | Complete — ACH-01 through ACH-05, ACH-08 and ACH-09 shipped                                                                     | Verified milestones, attendance ranks, safe next-milestone progress, comeback recognition, a curated showcase, the complete privacy-filtered profile ledger and a crest and pigment per rank are live.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| 8     | Social core              | XL   | Complete — SOC-02 through SOC-09, with the gating PROF-10 and TRUST-04, shipped; SOC-10, SOC-11 and SOC-13 retained as candidates | Safe discovery, verified activity, bounded interaction and permissioned training partners are in production. `SOC-09` closes the committed group with fixed, rate-limited encouragements that do not create a conversation. Challenges are recorded, not committed, and nothing else waits on them. |
| 9     | Schedule and push        | XL   | Complete — SCHED-01–07, NOTIF-01–05 and NOTIF-08 shipped                                                                        | The installed PWA now schedules, delivers and lets the owner switch off every notification it sends.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| 10    | Routine ecosystem        | XL   | ROUT-04 through ROUT-11 and ROUT-15 shipped; ROUT-03 and ROUT-16 queued                                                         | Sharing, cloning, lineage, versions, discovery, dated blocks and their execution are live: the block in force on a date drives the schedule, the dashboard and a workout. `ROUT-03` still needs curated template content. `ROUT-16` is next: an explicit, bounded deload override on top of the plan `ROUT-15` resolves. |
| 11    | Complex infrastructure   | XL   | OFFLINE-01, MSG-01 through MSG-06                                                                                               | Offline conflict resolution, realtime delivery, moderation, and unread state are substantial systems. `MSG-06` is the single realtime transport both private messaging and community rooms read.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| 12    | Communities              | XL   | COMM-01 through COMM-08                                                                                                         | A persistent social space needs identity, privacy, blocking, moderation, verified activity and a realtime transport before it has anything to host. Decompose further before scheduling any of it.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| 13    | Develop layer            | XL   | LIB-01 through LIB-03, INTEL-05                                                                                                 | Content needs an ownership, authoring and moderation model before it can exist, and evidence-linked guidance needs content to point at. Training stays the measurable foundation throughout.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |

Order is dependency order, not priority: group 1 leads because it is already in
flight, not because it outranks the honesty fixes in group 2, which were a day of
frontend work and proceeded in parallel -- the six frontend-only ones and
`FIX-03` shipped on 2026-09-07. Group 3 sits ahead of group 4 because nothing in
it waits on a production rollout, while group 4 cannot reach users until
projection reads are enabled.

Groups 8 through 13 are the product's three layers in dependency order: TRAIN
(groups 1 to 7, 9 and 10), then CONNECT (groups 8, 11 and 12), then DEVELOP
(group 13). The layers are cumulative rather than successive: training remains
the measurable foundation of the product through every later group, and no
social or content item may weaken it.

**Groups 8, 11, 12 and 13 are not one initiative and must never be started as
one.** Identity, privacy, blocking and reporting (`PROF-10`), moderation
(`TRUST-04`), notifications (`NOTIF-01` through `NOTIF-05`) and reliable,
verified activity data (`DATA-02`, `SOC-03`) precede every system that depends
on them. Each `XL` in those groups is a subsystem: decompose it into
independently deliverable features with their own IDs before any of it enters
implementation, the way `SOC-12` was decomposed into the `COMM-*` family.

## Feature catalog

### Live workout experience

| ID      | Status        | Size | Feature                        | User-facing behavior                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     | Dependencies                                                     |
| ------- | ------------- | ---- | ------------------------------ | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------- |
| LIVE-01 | `SHIPPED`     | M    | Rest timer                     | Completing a set starts the exercise's stored rest duration with add 15 seconds, skip, sound/vibration, and a deadline that survives backgrounding.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      | Existing `restSeconds`; NOTIF-02 for locked-screen alerts        |
| LIVE-02 | `SHIPPED`     | S    | Screen wake lock               | Keep the display awake while an active session is in use, with graceful fallback where unsupported.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      | None                                                             |
| LIVE-03 | `SHIPPED`     | M    | Previous performance inline    | Show the matching set from the most recent comparable workout beneath the current input and highlight improvement.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       | Efficient previous-session query                                 |
| LIVE-04 | `SHIPPED`     | S    | RPE capture                    | Let users enter the RPE already represented by set logs and displayed in history.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        | Existing set-log field                                           |
| LIVE-05 | `SHIPPED`     | M    | Plate calculator               | The active session exposes one calculator per weighted exercise. It starts from the next incomplete set's target and the default training location, allows both to change, and returns the exact bar-plus-plates load or the closest inventory-bounded load without exceeding the target. The result is expressed per side in the account's kg/lb unit, handles a bare bar and warns when the bar itself exceeds the target. Shipped 2026-09-08 as a frontend-only consumer of PREF-01.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  | PREF-01                                                          |
| LIVE-06 | `SHIPPED`   | S    | Train another day on demand    | **Decision taken 2026-09-22, and it made the item smaller.** Quick Workout is replaced by reaching a capability that already exists rather than by a new one: `startSession` has no date check and `/routines/[id]` already starts any day of any routine, so training off-plan works end to end and is merely four taps deep. This surfaces it where the need arises -- `/workouts`, where the dead button was, and the dashboard when today is a rest day or its plan is done -- as a picker over the owner's own routine days. No backend work and nothing to author. The template direction was **rejected for this item**: it answers "I have no routine at all", which is onboarding and belongs to `ROUT-03`, and adopting it here would block a two-tap fix behind a curated-content project with no owning ID. Free-form ad-hoc sessions were **rejected outright**; see the retained decision. | FIX-04                                                           |
| LIVE-07 | `SHIPPED`     | M    | Live PR detection              | Completing a set now celebrates every weight, rep, volume and estimated-1RM frontier it crosses. The result is calculated against completed history plus other completed sets in the active session; identical autosaves do not repeat it, bodyweight work can earn only the truthful rep category, and weight-derived values display in the account's kg/lb preference. Shipped 2026-09-08 through `@sunsteel/contracts@0.9.0` and the BE/FE set-log mutation path.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     | DATA-01; `@sunsteel/contracts@0.9.0`; BE/FE mutation path        |
| LIVE-08 | `SHIPPED`     | M    | Progression transparency       | Finishing a workout now pauses before Dashboard when prescriptions advanced and names each exercise, the all-sets or per-set rule, performed/target reps, and old → new load in the account's kg/lb unit. Only completed sets can satisfy a progression rule. The finish response is retry-stable because each change is stored as an idempotent `PROGRESSION_CHANGED` event. Shipped 2026-09-09 through `@sunsteel/contracts@0.11.0`, BE migration/event writer and FE result dialog.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   | DATA-02 progression slice; `@sunsteel/contracts@0.11.0`          |
| LIVE-09 | `SHIPPED`     | M    | Session recap                  | Completing a workout now pauses on a recap with duration, unit-aware volume, completed sets, the session's final record frontiers, persisted progression changes, session notes and duration/volume/set deltas against the latest completed session for the same preserved routine day. The same stable recap can be reopened from workout history; first sessions and absent notes use honest empty states. Shipped 2026-09-09 through `@sunsteel/contracts@0.12.0`, a reconstructible BE recap read/finish response and FE finish/history views.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       | DATA-01; DATA-02 progression slice; `@sunsteel/contracts@0.12.0` |
| LIVE-10 | `SHIPPED`     | M    | Abandoned-session recovery     | After 48 hours without activity, require the owner to Resume, finish using completed sets, or discard the session. Sessions are no longer auto-aborted in the background; Resume refreshes `lastActivityAt`, and the detailed set log is fetched only when the recovery prompt is needed. Shipped 2026-09-07 across FE/BE without a contract publish.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    | Existing `lastActivityAt`; FE/BE behavior change                 |
| LIVE-11 | `SHIPPED`     | L    | Exercise substitution          | Recommend alternatives by muscles and equipment and apply them to this session or optionally to the routine.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             | EXER-05, DATA-04                                                 |

| LIVE-13 | `CANDIDATE`   | M    | Warm-up ramp generator         | Generate non-counting ramp sets from the working weight, bar, and available plates.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      | LIVE-05, LIVE-12                                                 |
| LIVE-14 | `CANDIDATE`   | L    | Supersets and circuits         | Group exercises, rotate through them by round, and apply rest at the correct point. This is the session half of one capability: `ROUT-12` and its schema come first and this item executes what they store.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              | ROUT-12, LIVE-01                                                 |
| LIVE-15 | `CANDIDATE`   | M    | Faster set editing             | Duplicate a set, copy previous values, insert another set, or reorder session-only work.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 | LIVE-06                                                          |
| LIVE-16 | `SHIPPED`     | M    | Session notes                  | Shipped 2026-09-23. The live session screen has a Workout Note section and a note control on every exercise; both save to the session, never to the routine, and the routine's own exercise note is shown read-only as "Routine note" in the header and in the dialog. Until now the only note control on that screen wrote the routine's note, so a remark about today replaced the standing instruction for every later workout. Notes can be written or changed afterwards from the workout's history page too, with no window, because they feed nothing derived; a discarded workout refuses them. Exercise notes merge by slot so two saves cannot overwrite each other, are named in the recap as the exercise was performed (a swapped slot by its substitute), and travel with a shared link only when its owner shared notes.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       | CT/BE session note contracts                                     |
| LIVE-17 | `SHIPPED`     | L    | Post-workout correction window | Shipped 2026-09-23. The owner's most recent completed workout can be corrected on its history page for 48 hours after it ends and until another workout starts, at most ten times: weight, reps, RPE and whether each logged set was done. The server re-derives everything that workout fed in one locked transaction -- session totals, the day and week rollups, the projection, records (a record that no longer beats every other workout returns to the best earlier set, or to nobody), milestones it unlocked, its own load changes written back to the routine, the notification that counted them, and the activity entries built from them, whose reactions and comments go with a fact that did not happen. A load change the owner has since edited by hand is kept and reported. Every saved correction is an append-only row with the values before and after, shown on the page and included in the export.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                | DATA-02                                                          |
| LIVE-18 | `CANDIDATE`   | M    | Gym mode                       | Offer larger controls, stronger contrast, and fewer secondary actions during training. Deliver it as the session-screen acceptance criteria of `A11Y-02` rather than a separate mode with its own toggle and stored state: the intervention is the same one, and a distinct mode needs a product reason that does not exist yet.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         | A11Y-02                                                          |
| LIVE-19 | `SHIPPED`     | S    | Discard an in-progress session | The active-session action card now lets the owner discard at any time. A dedicated destructive confirmation clearly distinguishes discard from finish, warns that the session and saved sets will not enter history, disables both actions while the existing `ABORTED` mutation is pending, and reports success or failure before returning to the dashboard. Shipped 2026-09-08 without backend or contract changes because the endpoint, status and cache invalidation already existed.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               | FE                                                               |

### Analytics and historical-data foundation

| ID      | Status    | Size | Feature                      | User-facing or architectural outcome                                                                                      | Dependencies                 |
| ------- | --------- | ---- | ---------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------- |
| DATA-01 | `SHIPPED` | L    | Persistent personal records  | Store record events when they occur instead of deriving all records from the user's lifetime set-log table on every read. | BE/CT migration and backfill |
| DATA-02 | `SHIPPED` | L    | Training-event stream        | Persist session completions, PRs, streak milestones, progression changes, and achievements as addressable events.         | BE/CT schema                 |
| DATA-03 | `SHIPPED` | L    | Daily and weekly rollups     | Store volume, completed sets, active days, and muscle-group totals for fast progress views.                               | BE schema and backfill       |
| DATA-04 | `SHIPPED` | L    | Routine snapshot per session | Preserve the routine/day/exercise prescription used at training time so future edits cannot rewrite history.              | BE/CT schema                 |
| DATA-05 | `SHIPPED` | L    | Historical backfill          | Generate initial records, events, rollups, and snapshots from existing sessions with repeatable migration logic.          | DATA-01 through DATA-04      |

Together, `DATA-01` through `DATA-05` provide the durable data model and
backfill needed to close
[`TD-27`](technical-debt.md#td-27).
The technical-debt entry owns the query-cost evidence and closure criteria;
this roadmap owns the product and cross-repository delivery sequence.

**Delivery verified 2026-09-14.** DATA-01, DATA-03, DATA-04 and DATA-05 first
shipped on 2026-09-07. Production comparison passed for all seven accounts;
repeated backfill was idempotent, snapshot-safe relations were migrated and the
API legacy fallback was removed. See the
[TD-27 closure record](../history/td27-closure-2026-09-07.md). DATA-02 then
completed through `LIVE-08` and `ACH-01`: `TrainingEventType` now persists
`SESSION_COMPLETED`, `PERSONAL_RECORD`, `PROGRESSION_CHANGED`,
`STREAK_MILESTONE` and `ACHIEVEMENT_UNLOCKED`. New sessions award milestones
inside the idempotent projection writer, while the owner achievement read
reconciles pre-feature history into immutable, explicitly backfilled events.

### Progress

| PROG-01 | `SHIPPED`   | L    | Exercise strength trends        | Progress now selects among trained lifts and plots the best-set load and estimated-1RM record frontier over 30 days, 90 days, 6 months, 1 year or all time. A carried-in baseline keeps bounded ranges truthful; exact record rows retain date, load and reps. The read reconstructs persisted `PERSONAL_RECORD` events through a partial exercise/date index and caps responses at 500 changes instead of scanning lifetime set logs. Shipped 2026-09-10 through `@sunsteel/contracts@0.18.0`, a BE index migration and hand-rolled FE SVG charts.                                                                                                                                                                                                                           | DATA-01, DATA-03; `@sunsteel/contracts@0.18.0` |

Each card preserves its historical routine/day, actual sets and RPE, session note, prescription note and any automatic progression change; aborted sessions with real completed work remain visible, while incomplete sets do not become performance. Shipped 2026-09-11 through `@sunsteel/contracts@0.19.0`, indexed BE reads and the existing FE `/progress` destination.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                | DATA-04; `@sunsteel/contracts@0.19.0`          |

Each day carries one mark, the strongest of its entries: completed, in progress, ended early, not logged (never "missed"), planned or rest day. The month states its totals and on how many of its days so far a workout was completed. Recovery days are the planned rest days of `SCHED-07`; no day is judged against an invented target. Previous and next months page through history, and the section links to the Schedule. Shipped 2026-09-15 in the frontend over existing reads.                                                                                                                                                                                                 | DATA-02, SCHED-01                              |

Exercises and sets match through stable snapshot identities rather than mutable names; skipped work plus exercises added or removed between executions stay explicit, while one-session days and absent notes use honest empty states. The read caps routine-day discovery at 100 and loads only the two selected sessions. Shipped 2026-09-13 through `@sunsteel/contracts@0.22.0` without a migration or backfill.                                                                                                                                         | DATA-04; `@sunsteel/contracts@0.22.0`          |
| PROG-07 | `SHIPPED`   | M    | Record and progression timeline | Progress now interleaves persisted personal-record and automatic-progression events in a stable cursor-paginated feed, filterable by event type. Each row preserves its historical routine/day and session recap, explains whether a best set was the first recorded frontier, a heavier load or more reps at the same load, and expands exact per-set prescription changes with the stored rule evidence. The owner-scoped read defaults to 20 events, caps pages at 50 and never scans lifetime set logs. Shipped 2026-09-13 through `@sunsteel/contracts@0.23.0` without a migration or backfill. The four transient LIVE-07 celebration categories are not reconstructed as separate historical events because only the best-set `PERSONAL_RECORD` frontier is persisted. | DATA-01, DATA-02; `@sunsteel/contracts@0.23.0` |

Progress evaluates them from bounded current-week rollups, the active streak projection, persisted records and the owner's body weight, showing exact current, target and remaining gap in the account's unit. At-most body-weight goals intentionally omit a percentage when a bounded bar would misrepresent direction. Each row states the best set, its estimated 1RM and the closest set since; a lift whose estimated 1RM rose is never flagged, the thresholds are printed above the list, and the exercise page repeats the note. Numbers only, never a cause. It must not conclude fatigue, recovery state or any other cause: the previous wording, "flag possible fatigue", named a cause the product cannot observe and contradicted the retained decision. `INTEL-02` is the action half and reads these signals.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         | LIVE-04, DATA-03, PROG-09                      |

Use lightweight CSS or hand-rolled SVG for initial progress visuals. Do not
restore a large charting dependency without measuring the installed PWA's
cold-start impact and showing that simpler rendering is insufficient.

### Exercise library

| ID      | Status      | Size | Feature                        | User-facing behavior                                                                                                                                                                   | Dependencies                    |
| ------- | ----------- | ---- | ------------------------------ | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------- |
| EXER-01 | `SHIPPED`   | M    | Personal exercise page         | Show routines using the exercise, recent performances, best set, estimated 1RM, and progression history.                                                                               | PROG-01, PROG-02                |
| EXER-02 | `SHIPPED`   | M    | Catalog browsing               | Filter the catalog by name, muscle, equipment (one item or everything listed at the default gym), movement pattern and training history. Stars arrived with `EXER-07`.                 | EXER-09                         |
| EXER-03 | `CANDIDATE` | XL   | Instructions and cues          | Provide setup, execution, common mistakes, and appropriate safety notes.                                                                                                               | LIB-01 content model            |
| EXER-04 | `CANDIDATE` | XL   | Muscle visualization and media | Display targeted muscles and concise demonstrations where reliable assets exist.                                                                                                       | LIB-01; curated assets/licences |
| EXER-05 | `SHIPPED`   | M    | Alternatives                   | Suggest substitutions based on movement, primary muscles, and available equipment.                                                                                                     | EXER-09                         |
| EXER-06 | `CANDIDATE` | L    | Custom exercises               | Let users create private catalog entries with muscles, equipment, and notes.                                                                                                           | BE/CT ownership model           |
| EXER-07 | `SHIPPED`   | L    | Favorites and recents          | Star exercises privately and see starred, then recently trained, exercises first in the routine wizard's pickers; the catalog filters by stars. Quick Workout stays hidden (`FIX-04`). | Starred-exercise model          |
| EXER-08 | `CANDIDATE` | L    | Catalog moderation             | Review duplicates, naming, instructions, and promoted user submissions.                                                                                                                | Administrative tooling          |
| EXER-09 | `SHIPPED`   | L    | Exercise metadata expansion    | Extend the catalog with movement pattern, equipment detail, substitution grouping and instruction/media fields so dependent features stop blocking on undefined data.                  | BE/CT schema; catalog backfill  |

The first Exercises release should emphasize the user's existing training data.
Instructions and media make this a content project as well as an engineering one.

`EXER-09` shipped the data those items were waiting on: every catalog exercise
now carries a movement pattern, a compound/isolation mechanic, the equipment it
needs (in the same lowercase vocabulary as `PREF-01` location equipment) and a
substitution group of near-identical exercises that never crosses movement
patterns. `EXER-02`, `EXER-05` and `ROUT-10` are no longer blocked on catalog
data. `instructions` and `mediaUrl` exist but stay empty until `EXER-03` and
`EXER-04` supply real content; nothing should render them before then.

`EXER-02` shipped the first Exercises page on 2026-09-14 as catalog browsing
only: filters for name, muscle, equipment, movement pattern and "Trained by me",
with each exercise's last training date. It adds no backend read, because the
existing performance history already lists every exercise with completed work.
The favorites filter in the original description was deliberately left out:
it needs the user-exercise preference model owned by `EXER-07`, which does not
exist. The five favorite exercises on the `PROF-05` training identity are
public-facing profile metadata, not a browsing preference, and were not reused
as a substitute.

`EXER-01` shipped the personal exercise page the same day. It composes the
existing reads: catalog facts, the owner's routine days that program the
exercise, the record frontier for the best set and estimated 1RM, paginated
performance history, and the progress timeline narrowed to the exercise. The
narrowing was the one missing read, so `GET /workouts/progress/timeline` gained
an optional `exerciseId` (`@sunsteel/contracts@0.31.0`) instead of the page
reconstructing progression from whichever performance pages happened to load.

`EXER-07` then shipped favorites as private **stars**. The name is deliberate:
the `PROF-05` training identity already has public "favorite exercises", and
two different favorites would blur a private working preference into profile
metadata. Stars need their own `StarredExercise` model and migration, so the
item grew from `M` to `L`. Recents need no storage: they come from the
trained-exercise list the catalog already reads. Quick Workout, the second
surface in the original description, stays hidden under `FIX-04` until
`LIVE-06` surfaces the existing routine-day picker.

### Routines and programming

| ID      | Status      | Size | Feature                        | User-facing behavior                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                | Dependencies                         |
| ------- | ----------- | ---- | ------------------------------ | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------ |
| ROUT-03 | `QUEUED`    | M    | Starter templates              | Open curated programs in the existing wizard as fully editable drafts for members who do not yet have a routine. It remains onboarding content with no owning library; `LIVE-06` explicitly does not wait on it.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    | Curated templates (no owning ID yet) |
| ROUT-04 | `SHIPPED`   | L    | Routine sharing                | Each routine carries its own `PRIVATE`/`FOLLOWERS`/`PUBLIC` visibility, and a revocable private link opens it for anyone holding the link whatever that setting says -- the `SOC-07` session-share pattern, token-only and permanently revocable. **Two rules apply and the narrower wins:** the account-level `PROF-06` routines rule caps each routine, so a per-routine switch can never widen a privacy setting made elsewhere, and the owner is told when it is being capped. **What travels is the prescription and only the prescription** -- days, exercises, sets, reps, rest, progression and target loads -- never sessions, records or notes. The payload is `captureRoutineSetup`, the shape `ROUT-08` versions already store, chosen because it has no field a log could occupy even by mistake. A routine a viewer may not read answers 404 rather than 403, so it cannot be distinguished from one that does not exist, and the public read sends `Cache-Control: no-store`. Shipped 2026-09-17 through `@sunsteel/contracts@0.49.0` and migration `20260917200000_routine_sharing`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                | PROF-06                              |
| ROUT-05 | `SHIPPED`   | M    | Routine cloning                | Any routine a viewer may read — through a link or through `ROUT-04` visibility — is saved as a routine of their own by `POST /routines/clones`, which takes exactly one source and goes through the reads that already exist, so a clone can never see more than a reader can. **What is copied is the shared `RoutineVersionSetup` put back through `setupToRoutineUpdate`**, the pair `ROUT-08` restores already use, rather than a third projection of a routine: a field able to carry the original owner's training would have to be added to a shape with nowhere to put one. The clone starts `PRIVATE` with no links, versions, favourite or completed state, because inheriting `PUBLIC` would republish somebody else's programme without anyone choosing to, and an exercise withdrawn from the catalog is refused by name instead of cloned into a day that cannot be trained. Lineage stays with `ROUT-06`. Shipped 2026-09-18 through `@sunsteel/contracts@0.50.0`; no migration.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     | ROUT-04                              |
| ROUT-06 | `SHIPPED`   | M    | Routine lineage                | A clone records the routine and the author it came from, on the clone only -- the source is never told it was copied. **What a viewer may be told is decided by the same `canViewRoutine` every other routine read uses:** a clone of a routine the viewer may not read still says it is a clone and reports the source as hidden, because otherwise a public clone would become a way to discover that a private routine exists and who wrote it. A deleted source, a deleted author and a block all reach that same state, and deleting a source never deletes somebody else's copy. Shipped 2026-09-18 through `@sunsteel/contracts@0.51.0` and migration `20260918140000_blocks_reports_lineage`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               | ROUT-05, PROF-03                     |
| ROUT-07 | `SHIPPED`   | L    | Routine discovery              | `GET /routines/discover` browses the routines other members shared. **It is not a new visibility tier:** the candidate set is decided by the shipped `canViewRoutine`, so it lists exactly what the viewer could already open from a profile, and `PROF-10` removes both parties of a block from each other's results. **Four of the six facets are derived and two are declared.** Days, muscle focus and equipment come from the routine's own exercises through the `EXER-09` catalog, and duration from `ROUT-10`'s `estimateDaySeconds` ported to the server, so the wizard and discovery cannot quote different numbers; none of them is stored, because a denormalised facet drifts the moment an edit misses its recompute. Goal and experience level are **owner-declared fields on the routine**, reusing the `PROF-05` enums: they are claims only an author can make, and deriving them from that author's training identity would attribute a person's goals to a programme. An undeclared one is unknown rather than a wildcard, so a routine whose author said nothing never answers that filter. Equipment reads as what the **viewer has**. The scan is capped and the response reports truncation. Shipped 2026-09-19 through `@sunsteel/contracts@0.52.0` and migration `20260919100000_routine_classification`. | ROUT-04, EXER-02                     |
| ROUT-08 | `SHIPPED`   | L    | Routine versions               | The routine detail page saves the routine as it is now under an optional name, compares any saved version with the current routine day by day (days by weekday or position, exercises by catalog exercise) and restores it after saying what would change. A version is an immutable copy of the whole setup, loads included; restoring first saves the setup it replaces, so it can be undone, keeps the routine and its history, and is refused while a session of the routine is in progress. A routine keeps up to 20 versions. Shipped 2026-09-15 through `@sunsteel/contracts@0.38.0` and migration `20260915150000_routine_versions`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        | DATA-04                              |
| ROUT-09 | `SHIPPED`   | L    | Dated training blocks          | Add named, non-overlapping date ranges to one routine and author each block as an immutable `RoutineVersionSetup`, starting from the routine as it exists now or from one of its saved `ROUT-08` versions. The routine remains the editable baseline; changing or deleting a future block never rewrites that baseline, and a block that has started is replaced by a new revision rather than edited in place. The routine detail page owns the ordered timeline and exact setup diff. This slice stores and edits the plan but does not silently switch the schedule or a session prescription; execution belongs to `ROUT-15`. Shipped 2026-09-23 through `@sunsteel/contracts@0.59.0` and migration `20260923120000_routine_training_blocks`. | ROUT-08, SCHED-01                    |
| ROUT-10 | `SHIPPED`   | M    | Quality summary                | Before saving, show weekly muscle-group sets, equipment, estimated duration, and likely imbalances.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 | EXER-09                              |
| ROUT-11 | `SHIPPED`   | L    | Flexible day identity          | A routine is weekly (each day on its weekday, as before) or a rotation of up to seven days that runs in order on any weekday, and every day may carry its own name. The builder offers rotation presets, reordering and a name per day, and switching modes keeps every day and its exercises. A rotation offers the day after the last completed session (an aborted one does not advance it) on the routine card, the detail page and the dashboard, and any of its days can still be started. Days read by their name, weekday or rotation letter everywhere, history included, while older snapshots keep their weekday. Shipped 2026-09-14 through `@sunsteel/contracts@0.36.0` and migration `20260914230000_rotation_routines`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              | BE/CT routine model                  |
| ROUT-12 | `CANDIDATE` | M    | Superset/circuit configuration | Group routine exercises and define order within a round. This is the schema and builder half of one capability and **precedes** `LIVE-14`, which executes the grouping during a session. It no longer depends on `LIVE-14`: the two used to depend on each other, which left both unstartable.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      | BE/CT schema                         |
| ROUT-13 | `CANDIDATE` | M    | Routine import/export          | Back up and exchange programs through a stable versioned format.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    | EXPORT-01                            |
| ROUT-14 | `DEFERRED`  | XL   | Collaborative editing          | Allow an invited coach or collaborator to suggest or make changes with an audit trail.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              | COACH-01, DATA-02                    |
| ROUT-15 | `SHIPPED`   | L    | Phase-aware routine execution  | Shipped 2026-09-23. contracts' `resolveRoutinePlan` is the one resolver of what a routine trains on a date -- its active `ROUT-09` block's schedule and days, else the baseline -- used by the frontend schedule (segmented at block boundaries), dashboard, routine card and page, routine-list start and Train another day, and by the backend start, NOTIF-04 reminders, the SOC-08 partner count and SCHED-04/05 moves and skips. Each block revision is also written as a working copy of real days, exercises and sets, so a session of a block day logs against them; progression raises the block's loads and never the baseline's, and a new revision restarts from its authored loads. A start refuses a day outside the plan in force on the owner's local date, and the session and its snapshot record the block id, series, revision and name. A rotation continues from its own plan's sessions, so a block starts on its first day and the baseline resumes where it left off. The live session screen now trains the session's snapshot day. Owner decisions: progression inside the block, only the block's days while it is in force, reminders and the partner schedule follow it. | ROUT-09, ROUT-11, DATA-04            |
| ROUT-16 | `IN_PROGRESS` | M    | Temporary deload override      | Let the owner create a bounded, explicitly lighter override from the currently effective prescription, review the exact set/load/repetition differences and end it early. The override is a dated prescription with a `DELOAD` label, never an inferred recovery diagnosis and never a mutation of the routine or its training block. It cannot overlap another temporary override, and `ROUT-15` remains the sole resolver that decides what a session snapshots. Automatic suggestions remain `INTEL-02`. | ROUT-15; `INTEL-02` remains optional |
